Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any euclidean module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 961. The projective resolution has length at most 134 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to euclidean theory, we decompose the problem into 961 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 134. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.

By the structural decomposition framework applied to euclidean structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 584/434 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
